# Phase 2 additions (local, not published)

3671 repos, each 1,000+ stars, maintained in the last 12 months, classified by an agent and spot-checked by a judge.

## Agent Frameworks & Orchestration

**Core frameworks**

- [AstrBotDevs/AstrBot](https://github.com/AstrBotDevs/AstrBot) - An AI agent assistant and development framework integrating LLMs, plugins, and messaging platforms.

## Learning Path

**Foundations**

- [tensorflow/models](https://github.com/tensorflow/models) - A collection of models and examples built with the TensorFlow machine learning framework.
- [scikit-learn/scikit-learn](https://github.com/scikit-learn/scikit-learn) - A foundational Python library for machine learning algorithms and data analysis.
- [keras-team/keras](https://github.com/keras-team/keras) - A high-level deep learning API for building and training neural networks.
- [datawhalechina/happy-llm](https://github.com/datawhalechina/happy-llm) - A course on building large language models from scratch.
- [harvard-edge/cs249r_book](https://github.com/harvard-edge/cs249r_book) - Machine learning textbook.
- [AccumulateMore/CV](https://github.com/AccumulateMore/CV) - Comprehensive deep learning notes covering PyTorch, agents, and large models.
- [spmallick/learnopencv](https://github.com/spmallick/learnopencv) - Learning resources and examples for OpenCV, covering computer vision and deep learning.
- [recommenders-team/recommenders](https://github.com/recommenders-team/recommenders) - Best practices and algorithms for building recommendation systems.
- [liyupi/ai-guide](https://github.com/liyupi/ai-guide) - A comprehensive guide and resource collection for AI, covering agents, LLMs, RAG, and development frameworks.
- [fengdu78/deeplearning_ai_books](https://github.com/fengdu78/deeplearning_ai_books) - Notes and resources for Andrew Ng's deeplearning.ai courses.
- [ZJU-LLMs/Foundations-of-LLMs](https://github.com/ZJU-LLMs/Foundations-of-LLMs) - A book for learning the foundational concepts of Large Language Models.

## Local Models, Inference & Hardware

**Serving**

- [tinygrad/tinygrad](https://github.com/tinygrad/tinygrad) - A micrograd-inspired deep learning framework.
- [dmlc/xgboost](https://github.com/dmlc/xgboost) - Gradient boosting library.
- [fastai/fastai](https://github.com/fastai/fastai) - Deep learning library.
- [QwenLM/Qwen3](https://github.com/QwenLM/Qwen3) - Large language model.
